6   Vitasoy International Holdings Ltd. 維他奶國際集團有限公司
          Chairman’s Statement
          主 席報告




Vitasoy Group revenue decreased by 3% in FY2025/2026, primarily         維他奶集團於二零二五╱二零二六財政年度的
due to weaker performance in the Chinese Mainland where the             收入減少 3%，主要由於中國內地業務受到傳統
business was affected by softness in the general trade channel,         零售渠道疲弱表現影響所致，惟全渠道的增長
partially mitigated by encouraging growth in the Omni channels.         令人鼓舞並抵銷部分有關影響。

The Hong Kong Operation’s revenue declined slightly due to slower       澳門特別行政區及維他天地業務需求放緩，使
demand in the Macau SAR and Vitaland Group businesses, while            香港業務收入輕微下降，但在植物奶及茶類別
retaining strong market leadership in both the plant milk and tea       均能在香港特別行政區保持強勁的市場領導地
categories in the Hong Kong SAR. Australia/New Zealand and              位。澳洲╱新西蘭及新加坡收入持續穩健增長，
Singapore continued to deliver solid revenue growth, with the former    前 者 經 營 虧 損 收 窄（不 包 括 物 業、廠 房 及 設 備
narrowing the operating losses (excluding an impairment charge          減 值 支 出），後 者 則 恢 復 盈 利。於 集 團 層 面，
related to property, plant and equipment) and the latter returning to   我們透過將更多生產整合至東莞廠房，提升資
profitability. At the Group level, we improved asset utilisation and    產 使 用 率 及 廠 房 效 率。儘 管 Vitasoy Australia
plant efficiency by consolidating more production at our Dongguan       Products Pty. Ltd. 錄得減值支出（「VAP 減值」），
factory. We also strengthened the balance sheet through the             我們亦透過出售維他奶（上海）有限公司持有的
disposal of land and buildings held by Vitasoy (Shanghai) Company       土地及樓宇（「VSL 出售事項」）強化資產負債表。
Limited (“VSL Disposal”), despite the impairment charge recorded by
Vitasoy Australia Products Pty. Ltd. (“VAP Impairment”).

Profit attributable to equity shareholders of the Company increased     本 公 司 股 權 持 有 人 應 佔 溢 利 增 加 17%。撇 除
by 17%. Excluding the VSL Disposal and VAP Impairment, profit           VSL 出 售 事 項 及 VAP 減 值，本 公 司 股 權 持 有 人
attributable to equity shareholders of the Company decreased by         應 佔 溢 利 減 少 4%。基 於 本 集 團 的 財 務 表 現，
4%. Based on the Group’s financial performance, and having regard       並考慮到本集團的現金狀況以及維持穩定派息
to the Group’s cash position and its policy of maintaining a stable     率的政策，董事會已建議派發二零二五╱二零
payout ratio, the Board of Directors has proposed a final dividend of   二六財政年度末期股息每股普通股港幣 13.5 仙，
HK13.5 cents per ordinary share for FY2025/2026, which reflects         該 建 議 亦 已 反 映 VSL 出 售 事 項 的 影 響，惟 須 待
the VSL Disposal, subject to the shareholders’ approval at the Annual   股東於將於二零二六年八月二十四日舉行的股
General Meeting to be held on 24th August 2026. Together with the       東週年大會上批准。連同中期股息每股普通股
interim dividend of HK4.0 cents per ordinary share, this brings the     港幣 4.0 仙，二零二五╱二零二六財政年度的股
total dividend for FY2025/2026 to HK17.5 cents per ordinary share.      息總額為每股普通股港幣 17.5 仙。

In addition to the usual dividend distribution, the Company also        除一般股息派發外，本公司於本財政年度亦進
undertook a share buy-back programme during the financial year to       行股份回購計劃，以提升回報並為股東創造額
enhance returns and create additional value for shareholders.           外價值。

    Outlook                                                                    展望
    In FY2026/2027, our priority will be to leverage our brand equity,         於二零二六╱二零二七財政年度，我們將優先
    portfolio strengths and marketing campaigns, as well as our upgraded       發揮品牌價值、產品組合優勢及市場推廣活動
    commercial capabilities, particularly in availability expansion, channel   的效益，以及已提升的商業能力，尤其是在擴
    focus and in-store sales execution. We will also strive to secure          大供應覆蓋、聚焦渠道及店內銷售執行方面。
    growth in the general trade channel and accelerate our growth in           我們亦將致力在傳統零售渠道取得增長，並透
    the fast-growing Omni channel, including snack chains, via increased       過加強定制化方針及產品差異化，推動正快速
    customisation and differentiation.                                         擴張的全渠道（包括零食連鎖店）的增長。

    We will continue supporting the VITASOY and VITA brands to                 我們將繼續推廣維他奶及維他品牌，確保在追
    ensure they retain their equity and relevance for shoppers seeking         求和選擇優質及更健康植物性飲品的消費者心
    high quality, healthier plant based beverage choices. We will              目中，保持品牌價值及意義。我們亦將維持高
    also maintain our high standards of Environmental, Social and              水平的環境、社會及管治表現，並獲本地及國
    Governance (ESG) performance, as recognised by leading local and           際主要評級機構認可。
    international ratings.

    While geopolitical and macroeconomic uncertainties are likely to           儘管不明朗的地緣政治及宏觀經濟環境可能持
    persist, we are confident in our Group’s fundamentals and long             續，我們對本集團的基本面及未來的長遠定位
    term positioning for the future. We will remain firmly committed           充滿信心。我們將堅定致力於實現可持續增長，
    to delivering sustainable growth that benefits our shareholders,           惠及股東、僱員、業務夥伴以及我們所服務社
    employees, business partners and customers in the communities we           區的客戶。

10   Vitasoy International Holdings Ltd. 維他奶國際集團有限公司
          Group Chief Executive Officer’s Report/Business Review
          集 團行政總裁報告╱業務回顧




In FY2025/2026, Group revenue declined by 3%, attributable                 於二零二五╱二零二六財政年度，本集團收入
mainly to slow first half performance in the Chinese Mainland that         下降 3%，主要由於中國內地業務於上半年表現
subsequently improved in the second half. VITASOY continued                緩慢，其後於下半年有所改善。維他奶在植物
to outperform in plant milk categories and recorded sustained              奶類別繼續表現出眾，並於植物奶及豆奶分部
market share in both the plant milk and soymilk segments. In tea,          均保持穩定市場份額。茶類方面，維他在推出
VITA regained momentum in the sweetened tea category following             維他鴨屎香檸檬茶後，於有糖茶品類重拾動力。
the launch of VITA Ya Shi Xiang Lemon Tea. Challenging market              傳統零售渠道的市場環境充滿挑戰，但全渠道
conditions in the general trade channel were partly offset by              錄得令人鼓舞的增長，抵銷部分有關影響，並
encouraging growth in the Omni channel, showing potential for              展現進一步加速增長的潛力。
further acceleration.

Hong Kong Operation revenue decreased by 3%, due to weaker                 澳門特別行政區及維他天地業務表現較弱令香
performance in the Macau SAR and Vitaland businesses. Operating            港 業 務 收 入 減 少 3%。在 嚴 謹 成 本 控 制 及 具 優
profit margin was broadly maintained at 12%, supported by                  勢的產品組合支持下，經營溢利率大致維持於
disciplined cost control and a favourable product mix. The Hong            12%。香港特別行政區業務憑藉穩固的核心業
Kong SAR business maintained its leadership share in both the              務及產品創新，在植物奶及茶類方面均保持領
plant milk and tea categories, bolstered by a solid core business and      先市場份額。
accretive product innovation.

In Australia and New Zealand, revenue grew by 4% and                       在 澳 洲 及 新 西 蘭，收 入 增 長 4%，生 產 趨 於 穩
manufacturing operations stabilised, laying a strong foundation for        定，奠定進一步增長的堅實基礎。儘管本財政

Financial Risk Management                                                 財務風險管理
The Group’s overall financial management policy focuses on                本集團的整體財務管理政策強調預測及管控風
anticipating, controlling and managing risks, and covering                險，涵蓋與本集團的相關業務直接有關的交易。
transactions directly related to the underlying businesses of the         為達致協同效益、效率及監控的目的，本集團
Group. For synergy, efficiency and control, the Group operates a          為其所有附屬公司實行中央現金及財政管理系
central cash and treasury management system for all subsidiaries.         統。各營運附屬公司一般以當地貨幣進行借貸，
Borrowings are normally taken out in local currencies by the              為當地投資項目提供資金及減低部份外匯風險。
operating subsidiaries to fund their investments and partially mitigate
foreign currency risks.


Potential Risk and Uncertainties                                          潛在風險及不明朗因素
The Company has implemented a comprehensive risk management               本公司在集團內實施全面的風險管理框架，採
framework across the Group to consistently anticipate, assess and         用一致的流程以預測、評估及減輕關鍵業務風
mitigate key business risks, as well as a risk governance structure to    險，以及採納風險管治架構，確保風險責任管
ensure risk ownership and proper oversight. In view of the rapidly        理得到落實和適當監督。鑑於營商環境瞬息萬
changing business environment, the Group Internal Audit and Risk          變，本集團內部審計及風險管理部因而加強提
Management Department has stepped up its efforts to improve               升關鍵風險指標，識別外來新興風險，促進關
key risk indicators, identify external emerging risks and facilitate      鍵採購決策風險審核。該等風險管理流程的詳
risk reviews for key purchasing decisions. Details of these risk          情刊載於本年報內「企業管治報告」的風險管理
management processes are covered in the Risk Management Process           程序一節。
section of the “Corporate Governance Report” in this Annual Report.

     Vitasoy is one of the world’s leading producers of tasty nutritious     八十多年以來，維他奶生產營養美味、以植物
     plant-based foods and beverages for over 80 years. In 1940, our         為本的食品及飲品，是世界首屈一指的生產商
     founder Dr. K.S. Lo was motivated to provide an abundant source of      之一。於一九四零年，我們的創始人羅桂祥博
     affordable nutrition to help improve the lives of others. Since then,   士深受啟發，為大眾提供豐富且價格相宜的營
     we have built on his principles to champion sustainable practices       養飲料，以改善大眾生活。自此我們秉承羅博
     across our business. We are leading the transition towards more         士的初心，在業務經營中倡導可持續性。公司
     sustainable plant-based nutrition. Our founding purpose continues       創辦的理念到今天仍然啟發著我們，繼續熱衷
     to inspire us today.                                                    推動植物性飲食潮流。

      Key Features or Mechanisms                       Implementation and Review of Effectiveness

      Directors Conflict of Interest Policy            The Company has policy and procedures in place to avoid any
      董事利益衝突政策                                         potential conflict of interests and not to weaken the objectivity
                                                       and integrity of the Board for decisions making. Under the
                                                       Policy, any Director has to declare his/her directorships outside
                                                       the Company, interests in controlling company, public office
                                                       appointment and any other interests that might be considered
                                                       prejudicial to his/her independence and has to declare his/her
                                                       interests in any transaction in which he/she may have interest and
                                                       abstain from voting on such transaction.
                                                       本公司已制定政策及程序以避免任何潛在利益衝突，且不會削弱
                                                       董事會決策的客觀性及誠信性。根據該政策，任何一位董事必須
                                                       申報其於本公司以外的董事職務、於控股公司中的權益、公職任
                                                       命及任何其他可能被認為有礙其獨立性的利益，並須申報其於可
                                                       能擁有利益的任何交易中的權益，並就該交易放棄投票。

      Open Board Culture                               INEDs in family businesses will generally play a more active role in
      開明的董事會文化                                         corporate governance area. Our Board culture under the leadership
                                                       of Executive Chairman, encourages questions and challenges from
                                                       directors in particular from INEDs who may hold different views
                                                       from the Executive Directors and Senior Management and their
                                                       comments and concerns will be closely followed up and addressed
                                                       by the Executive Directors and Senior Management.
                                                       家族企業中的獨立非執行董事一般會於企業管治方面發揮更為
                                                       積極的作用。在執行主席領導下，我們的董事會文化鼓勵董事（尤
                                                       其是獨立非執行董事）提出問題及質疑，彼等可能與執行董事及
                                                       高層管理人員持有不同觀點，而執行董事及高層管理人員將密切
                                                       跟進及應對彼等的意見及關注。

      No equity-based remuneration with                Director fees payable to Director, including INEDs, are fixed fees
      performance-related elements                     without any discretionary element nor they are remunerated with
      to INEDs                                         equity-based awards. Directors Fees are also determined by the
      獨立非執行董事未曾授予與表現績效相關                               R&N Committee and the Board with reference to the roles and
      的股本權益酬金                                          responsibilities taken up by the Director and benchmarking against
                                                       peers.
                                                       應付董事（包括獨立非執行董事）的董事袍金為固定數額，並無
                                                       任何酌情因素，而酬金亦無包括以股權為基礎的獎勵。董事袍金
                                                       亦由薪酬及提名委員會與董事會參照董事所承擔的角色及職責
                                                       以及行業標準而釐定。

                                                   有助評估獨立機制的成效，識別任何潛在不足之處以便進一步改進。


The Board, through its R&N Committee, had reviewed and                董事會已透過其薪酬及提名委員會檢討並認為
considered that the abovesaid key features or mechanisms are          上述主要特點或機制可有效確保董事會取得獨
effective in ensuring that independent views are conveyed to          立意見。董事會亦認為全體獨立非執行董事均
the Board. The Board also considered that all INEDs meet the          符合上市規則第 3.13 條所載的獨立指引。
independence guidelines set out in Rule 3.13 of the Listing Rules.

Officer
The roles and responsibilities of Executive Chairman and Group                     執行主席及集團行政總裁之角色及職責已清楚
Chief Executive Officer are clearly defined and separate with a                    界定及區分，只有在促進可持續發展、確保與
very few overlapping of roles and responsibilities in the areas of                 持份者的有效溝通，以及促進企業文化及秉持
promoting sustainable development, ensuring effective stakeholder                  正直及誠實等方面之角色及職責上部份重疊。
communication, and promoting corporate culture and uphold of                       執行主席負責領導董事會、監督其營運及確保
integrity and honesty. The Executive Chairman is responsible for                   決策符合本集團最佳利益。集團行政總裁負責
providing leadership to the Board, overseeing its operations and                   管理本集團業務、監督本集團對策略、目標及
ensuring that decisions align with the best interests of the Group.                政策的制定及成功實施。
The Group Chief Executive Officer is responsible for managing
the Group’s businesses, overseeing the formulation and successful
implementation of the Group’s strategies, objectives, and policies.

promotion of good corporate governance practices, and to act on the                及根據授權代表執行主席行事。
Executive Chairman’s behalf as delegated.

     Board Proceedings                                                         董事會程序
     The Board met four times in FY2025/2026 to discuss and approve            董事會於二零二五╱二零二六財政年度舉行四
     the Group’s long term strategic plan; to review and monitor the           次會議，包括討論及批准本集團之長期策略規
     financial and business performance of the Group and each of               劃；審視及監察本集團及各營運單位之財務及
     its operating units; to consider and approve annual budget, the           業務表現；考慮及審批年度預算及財務報告；
     financial reports; to review and approve sustainability report and        審閱及批准可持續發展報告及與企業管治及可
     various corporate governance and sustainability related policies          持續發展相關之各種政策及常規；並監察及審
     and practices; and to monitor and review the effectiveness of the         視本集團風險管理表現之成效。
     Group’s risk management performance.

     The Board and its Committees are supplied with full and timely            董事會及其委員會均適時獲得本公司所有相關
     information in relation to the Company, which enables them to             資料，以履行職責。本公司已有既定程序致使
     discharge their responsibilities. There are established procedures        董事在適當的情況下，為履行其職責及責任而
     for Directors to seek independent professional advice for them to         諮詢獨立專業意見，費用由本公司支付。
     discharge their duties and responsibilities, where appropriate, at the
     Company’s expenses.

Responsibilities of Directors                                            董事責任
Directors acknowledge their responsibilities for preparing the           董事對編製本公司財務報表承擔責任。董事並
financial statements of the Company. Directors are not aware of any      不知悉有任何重大不明朗之事件或情況可能會
material uncertainties relating to events or conditions that may cast    嚴重影響本公司持續經營之能力。二零二五╱
significant doubt upon the Company’s ability to continue as a going      二零二六財政年度之全年財務報表乃按持續經
concern. The Annual Financial Statements for FY2025/2026 are             營基準編製。本公司採納之所有新會計準則及
prepared on a going concern basis. All the new accounting standards      政策先經由審核委員會經周詳討論後批准，然
and policies adopted by the Company have been thoroughly                 後再提交董事會採納。
discussed and approved by the Audit Committee before adoption by
the Board.

The Group has adopted its own Code for Securities Transactions            本集團已就董事之證券交易採納一套行為守則
by Directors (the “Own Code”) on terms no less exacting than             （「自有守則」），其條款不比上市規則附錄 C3 所
the required standards set out in the Model Code for Securities           載之上市發行人董事進行證券交易之標準守則
Transactions by Directors of Listed Issuers contained in Appendix         所載列之規定標準寬鬆。本公司已對全體董事
C3 of the Listing Rules. Specific enquiries were made of all Directors    作出具體查詢，而彼等均確認於截至二零二六

     Evaluation of the Board and the Executive                                   董事會及執行主席之評審
     Chairman
     The Board believes that the evaluation of the effectiveness of the          董事會相信，對董事會及執行主席之成效進行
     Board and its Executive Chairman is an essential requirement of             評審，乃良好企業管治之重要要求，並自二零
     good corporate governance and has since FY2011/2012 adopted                 一一╱二零一二財政年度採納企業管治守則中
     the Recommended Best Practice under the CG Code of conducting               之建議最佳常規定期為董事會作出評審。
     regular Board evaluation exercises.

     The objective of the Board evaluation is to assist the Board in             董事會評估旨在協助董事會識別及解決其優勢
     identifying and addressing its strengths and weaknesses, and                及弱點，並突出董事會對自身之期望及有待進
     highlighting the Board’s expectations for itself and areas for further      一步改善之範疇。作為此評估過程的一部分，
     improvement. As part of this evaluation process, the Executive              本公司亦就執行主席作為董事會主席是否充分
     Chairman is also subject to evaluation on whether he has adequately         及有效地履行其角色及職責進行評估。董事會
     and effectively performed his roles and fulfilled his responsibilities as   致力定期檢討其表現及成效。
     the Chairman of the Board. The Board is committed to reviewing its
     own performance and effectiveness at a regular interval.

     A formal evaluation of the Board and the Executive Chairman was             已就二零二五╱二零二六財政年度進行董事
     conducted for FY2025/2026. The evaluation was facilitated by Sodali         會及執行主席的正式評估。評估由獨立管治顧
     & Co, an independent governance advisory firm, which previously             問公司 Sodali & Co 協助進行，該公司曾於二零
     supported the Board’s evaluation in 2022. The consistency of the            二二年支援董事會評估。兩個周期採用一致的
     review framework across the two cycles enables the Board to assess          檢討框架，使董事會能夠評估其表現隨時間的
     the evolution of its performance over time.                                 演變。

     The questionnaire was organised under the same thematic                     問卷採用與二零二二年相同的專題框架編排，
     framework adopted in 2022, supplemented by open-ended                       並設有開放式部分，邀請董事提供意見及建議。
     sections in which Directors were invited to provide commentary              為保持與先前檢討的一致性並實現縱向比較，
     and recommendations. To preserve consistency with the prior                 問卷內容與二零二二年大致相同，僅因應企業
     review and to enable longitudinal comparison, the questionnaire             管治守則及指引的最新發展作出有限更新。
     was substantively unchanged from 2022, save for a limited number
     of updates reflecting recent developments in the CG Code and
     guidance.

     All ten members of the Board completed the questionnaire,                   董 事 會 十 名 成 員 全 數 完 成 問 卷，回 應 率 為
     representing a 100% response rate. To safeguard the credibility             100%。為保障回應的可信度及坦誠性，各董事
     and candour of responses, the anonymity of individual Directors             的身份保持匿名，而執行主席不會回答與其自
     has been maintained, and the Executive Chairman was excluded                身表現相關的問題。外部顧問匯總及分析回應，
     from questions pertaining to his own performance. Responses were            並編製書面報告供董事會考慮。
     aggregated and analysed by the external advisor, who prepared a
     written report for the Board’s consideration.

Assessing risks and reviewing the effectiveness of internal controls is          本公司持續評估風險及檢討內部監控之成效。

designed to give reasonable assurance that:

•     Assets are prudently safeguarded;                                          •           資產獲得審慎保障；

•     Business activities are conducted in an effective and efficient            •           以有效及高效之方式進行業務活動；
      manner;

•     Financial reporting is accurate, timely and complete; and                  •           作出準確、適時及完整之財務報告；及

     Risk Governance Structure                                             風險管治架構
     The risk governance structure consists of three layers of roles and   風險管治架構包括下列三個層面之角色及職責：
     responsibilities as explained below:

      Layer                           Roles and Responsibilities
      層面                              職務及職責
      Oversight                       Audit Committee, acting on behalf of the Board
      監督                              •     Oversees the Company’s risk management and internal control on an ongoing
                                            basis.
                                      •     Oversees those risks that warrant the Committee’s attention and supervise risk
                                            management process as part of good corporate governance.
                                      代表董事會之審核委員會
                                      •     持續監督本公司之風險管理及內部監控。
                                      •     監督委員會需要注意之該等風險並監督風險管理程序，以配合良好企業管治。
                                      Group Internal Audit
                                      •    Conducts independent review on the effectiveness of risk management and
                                           internal control system and provides reasonable assurance to Audit Committee.
                                      集團內部審計
                                      •    對風險管理及內部監控系統之成效進行獨立審閱，並向審核委員會提供合理
                                           保證。

      Risk Monitoring & Review        Enterprise Risk Management Executive Committee & Entity CEOs
      風險監察及審閱                         •     Design, implement and monitor the risk management and internal control
                                            system.
                                      •     Provide assurance to the Audit Committee on the effectiveness of risk
                                            management and internal control system.
                                      企業風險管理執行委員會及地方公司行政總裁
                                      •     設計、實施及監察風險管理及內部監控系統。
                                      •     向審核委員會提供有關風險管理及內部監控系統成效之保證。
                                      Group Risk Management, an independent team under Group Internal Audit and Risk
                                      Management
                                      •    Supports the Board of Directors, Audit Committee and Enterprise Risk
                                           Management Executive Committee.
                                      •    Develops and implements risk management frameworks, policies, tools and
                                           methodologies with all subsidiaries.
                                      •    Monitors status of risk and communicate with the Executive Committee and
                                           Audit Committee on an ongoing basis.
                                      集團風險管理為集團內部審計及風險管理部門轄下之獨立團隊
                                      •    支援董事會、審核委員會及企業風險管理執行委員會。
                                      •    與所有附屬公司建立及執行風險管理框架、政策、工具及方法。
                                      •    持續監察風險狀況，並與執行委員會及審核委員會持續溝通。

      Risk & Control Ownership        Department Heads & Individual Employee
      風險及控制的責任                        •    Ownership of managing risks, executing control activities and identifying new
                                           risks in daily operation.
                                      部門主管及個別員工
                                      •    於日常營運中管理風險，執行監控活動及識別新風險。

Risk Management Process                                                   風險管理程序
Our risk management process is embedded in our daily operations,          我們之風險管理程序已納入日常營運範圍、財
financial activities, compliance, strategy development & business         務 活 動、合 規、策 略 發 展 與 業 務 規 劃、投 資 決
planning, investment decision and external factors. The processes         定及外界因素，程序如下：
are as follows:

•     Anticipate: Potential risks and threats that are related to         •   預 測：所 有 員 工 均 參 與 識 別 不 同 業 務 流
      different business processes and relevant to the Company’s              程及與本公司目標相關之潛在風險及威
      objectives will be anticipated and identified by all individual         脅。為 便 於 進 行 適 當 評 估，我 們 將 風 險
      staff. Risks are grouped into six clusters (including reputation,       分為六個範疇（包括信譽、策略、市場、
      strategy, market, liquidity, credit and operation) to facilitate        流動資金、信貸及營運）。
      the assessment appropriately.

•     Rate: Each identified risk is evaluated on the basis of impact      •   評 估：按 照 董 事 會 設 定 之 風 險 偏 好，評
      and likelihood consistent with the risk appetite set by the             估 每 項 經 識 別 風 險 的 影 響 及 可 能 性。風
      Board. The risk impact and likelihood will be rated according           險 之 影 響 及 可 能 性 將 根 據 過 往 記 錄、統
      to the historical records, statistical analyses and relevant            計 分 析、相 關 經 驗 及 管 理 層 之 專 業 判 斷
      experiences, specialist and expert judgments by management.             進行評級。集團風險管理促進評估流程，
      Group Risk Management facilitates the evaluation process                包括協助企業風險管理執行委員會及審
      including the summary discussion in the Enterprise Risk                 核委員會之討論。
      Management Executive Committee and Audit Committee.

•     Respond: A delegated management is assigned to each risk            •   控 制：各 項 風 險 將 配 對 專 責 管 理 人 員，
      as risk owner to fully respond on each risk. Risk management            成 為 風 險 負 責 人，以 有 效 管 理 風 險。風
      strategies and corresponding control owners are identified by           險負責人制定風險管理策略以及指派監
      risk owner to deploy appropriate control activities.                    控負責人，實施適當監控活動。

•     Regulate: We take a close monitoring on the current status of       •   監 察：我 們 密 切 監 控 風 險 現 況。各 風 險
      risk, which is being monitored through key risk indicators with         現況的結果取決於關鍵風險指標的預設
      defined thresholds, occurrence of related incidents, control            量 度 級 別 基 準、所 發 生 之 相 關 事 故、內
      effectiveness and audit results. Any change in current status           部 監 控 程 序 之 成 效 以 及 審 計 結 果。風 險
      of risk to a worsened level will trigger a react process through        現 況 倘 出 現 任 何 不 良 轉 變，均 會 啟 動 反
      creation of a mitigation plan.                                          應程序，展開減輕風險計劃。

•     React: When there is a change on the current status of risk,        •   緩 解：當 風 險 現 況 有 所 改 變，相 應 負 責
      the corresponding owner will coordinate mitigation plan with            人 將 協 調 減 輕 風 險 計 劃 之 行 動 細 節、負
      action details, responsible person and timeline to address the          責人及時間表，以處理風險情況。
      status.

•     Report: To support the oversight of the Board on the                •   通 報：風 險 報 告 內 容 及 風 險 管 理 框 架，
      effectiveness of Vitasoy’s risk management, risk reporting              均 獲 董 事 會 批 准，協 助 董 事 會 監 察 維 他
      requirement was approved by the Board of Directors under                奶 風 險 管 理 之 成 效。集 團 風 險 管 理 定 期
      risk management framework. Periodic risk report is provided             向企業風險管理執行委員會提供風險報
      to the Enterprise Risk Management Executive Committee                   告，監 控 減 輕 風 險 計 劃 及 現 時 風 險 管 理
      summarising the results of risk monitoring, mitigation                  實施之進度。此外，自推出管治、風險管
      plans and the current risk management implementation                    理 及 合 規 系 統（「 GRC 系 統」）以 來，企 業
      progress. Besides, since the launch of the Governance, Risk             風險管理執行委員會及風險負責人可透
      and Compliance System (“GRC System”), Enterprise Risk                   過相關系統指標獲得實時風險狀況。
      Management Executive Committee and risk owners are able
      to obtain real-time risk status through the relevant system
      dashboards.

      in nurturing a risk management culture and encouraging                  勵 風 險 知 情 營 運 的 核 心 支 柱。維 他 奶 定
      risk-informed operations. Vitasoy conducts regular risk                 期 為 員 工 進 行 風 險 管 理 培 訓，以 提 高 風
      management training for associates to raise risk awareness              險 意 識 及 提 升 應 變 事 故 和 危 機 的 能 力。
      and improve preparedness for incidents and crises. The scope            年內風險管理培訓的覆蓋範圍正擴展至
      of risk management training this year is broadening to cover            不 同 組 織 層 級 的 員 工，以 進 一 步 提 升 風
      associates at multiple organisational levels, with the aim of           險緩解能力。
      further enhancing risk mitigation capabilities.

•     Risk reviews for key purchases                                      •   為關鍵採購進行風險審視
      In addition to the regular risk management process, Vitasoy             除 日 常 的 風 險 管 理 流 程 外，維 他 奶 於 關
      maintains the risk review process in making key purchasing              鍵採購的決策過程中加入風險審視流程。
      decisions. The objective of the review is to ensure that critical       審視的目的是確保重大採購風險獲得充
      purchasing risks are adequately assessed and considered and             分的評估及考慮，從而提升採購決策質素。
      hence improve the quality of purchasing decisions.

•     Managing ESG risks with risk management framework                   •   透 過 風 險 管 理 框 架 以 管 理 環 境、社 會 及

      Vitasoy’s risk management activities. ESG risks have been               奶 風 險 管 理 活 動 的 重 點。環 境、社 會 及
      managed alongside other relevant business and operational               管治風險已與其他相關業務及營運風險
      risks under the risk management framework. The GRC system               一 併 在 風 險 管 理 框 架 下 進 行 管 理。GRC
      continues to be a key tool for providing timely and transparent         系 統 將 繼 續 作 為 重 要 工 具，為 環 境、社

     Inside Information                                                        內幕消息
     With respect to procedures and internal controls for the handling         有關處理及發放內幕消息之程序和內部監控方
     and dissemination of inside information, the Company is aware of          面，本公司知悉，根據上市規則及《證券及期貨
     the requirements under the applicable Listing Rules and Securities        條例》之適用規定以及首要原則，本公司在得悉
     and Futures Ordinance and the overriding principle that inside            內幕消息後，應於切實可行情況下盡快公佈有
     information should be announced as soon as reasonably practicable         關資料，除非有關資料屬於《證券及期貨條例》
     after such information comes to our attention unless it falls within      中任何安全港條文所界定者則另當別論。為遵
     any of the safe harbours provisions under the Securities and Futures      守監管體系下有關內幕消息披露規定，本公司
     Ordinance. The Company has had a system in place with established         已有一套擁有既定政策及程序之系統。董事會
     policies and procedures for complying with the Inside Information         已於二零一二年十一月二十七日採納披露合規
     disclosure requirements under the regulatory regime. The Board            政策，並於二零二一年十一月二十五日進一步
     has adopted a Disclosure Compliance Policy on 27th November               作出一些細微修訂。該政策乃參照證券及期貨
     2012 which has been further revised with a few housekeeping               事務監察委員會所頒佈之《內幕消息披露指引》
     amendments on 25th November 2021. The Policy has been                     而編製，旨在為董事及僱員提供有關評估、匯
     formulated by reference to the “Guidelines on Disclosure of Inside        報及發佈內幕消息、保密及遵守股份交易限制
     Information” issued by the Securities and Futures Commission.             之指引。本公司已於行為守則及員工手冊內訂
     The purpose of the Policy is to provide Directors and employees           明嚴禁未經授權披露或使用公司之機密資料以
     with guidelines on assessing, reporting and disseminating inside          及內幕消息。
     information, maintaining confidentiality and abiding shares dealing
     restrictions. The Company has included in its code of conduct and
     staff handbook a strict prohibition on the unauthorised disclosure or
     use of confidential and inside information.

     Dividend Policy                                                           股息政策
     The Board has adopted a Dividend Policy to provide guidance to            董事會已採納股息政策，就派發盈餘資金予股
     the Board and the management of the Company for distribution of           東向董事會及管理層提供指引。
     surplus funds to its shareholders.

     In determining the level of dividend payment, the following criteria      就決定所派付的股息水平，須計及下列準則及
     and consideration must be taken:                                          考慮因素：

     –     Sharing of success with shareholders                                －   與股東共享成果

     –     Liquidity and capital requirement                                   －   流動資金及資本要求

     –     Historical trend of dividend payout                                 －   過往股息派付之趨勢

     –     Benchmarking with competitors within the industry                   －   與業內競爭對手之標準比較

     –     Fulfilment of financial covenants                                   －   符合財務契約

     –     Taxation impact                                                     －   稅務影響

     –     Restrictions                                                        －   限制條件

     –     Other factors that may deem relevant                                －   其他視為相關之因素

     The Company intends to declare dividends to shareholders to align         本公司計劃配合扣除一次性事件影響後之股東
     with the fluctuation in profit attributable to shareholders net of one-   應佔溢利之波動向股東宣派股息，並可能除中
     off event impact and may declare special dividends from time to time      期或末期股息外不時宣派特別股息。董事會將
     in addition to interim or final dividends. The determination to pay       根據股息政策不時酌情決定是否派發股息以及
     dividends and level of dividend to be distributed will be subject to      所派發股息之水平。
     the discretion of the Board made in accordance with the Dividend
     Policy from time to time.

3 to the consolidated financial statements.

(1)   Share option scheme approved on 28th August 2012                     (1)   於二零一二年八月二十八日批准之購
                                                                                 股權計劃
On 28th August 2012, the Company adopted a share option scheme             本公司於二零一二年八月二十八日採納購股權
(the “2012 Share Option Scheme”). The purpose of the 2012 Share            計劃（「二零一二年購股權計劃」）。二零一二年
Option Scheme is to recognise and acknowledge the contributions            購股權計劃旨在肯定及表揚本公司或其任何附
which the Directors, executives and employees of the Company or            屬公司之董事、行政人員及僱員（「合資格參與
any of its subsidiaries (“Eligible Participants”) have made or will make   者」）對本集團已作出或將作出之貢獻。該計劃
to the Group. It provides Eligible Participants with the opportunity to    讓合資格參與者持有本公司股份，以激勵合資
own a personal stake in the Company with a view to motivating the          格參與者為本集團之利益提升其表現及效率；
Eligible Participants to excel in their performance and efficiency for     以及吸引及挽留所作出之貢獻對或將對本集團
the benefit of the Group; and attracting and retaining or otherwise        長遠增長有所裨益之合資格參與者，或與彼等
maintaining an ongoing relationship with the Eligible Participants         維持持續之關係。董事會可酌情向本公司或其
whose contributions are or will be beneficial to the long term growth      附屬公司之任何董事、行政人員或僱員授予購
of the Group. The Board may at its discretion grant options to any         股權。
Director, executive or employee of the Company or its subsidiaries.

The principal terms of the 2012 Share Option Scheme were as                二零一二年購股權計劃之主要條款如下：
follows:

–     The exercise price of the options is the higher of (a) the           －     購 股 權 之 行 使 價 為 以 下 之 較 高 者：(a) 股
      closing price of the shares on the Stock Exchange on the date              份於授出日期（當日必須為營業日）在聯
      of grant, which must be a business day; and (b) the average of             交 所 錄 得 之 收 市 價；及 (b) 股 份 於 緊 接 授
      the closing prices of the shares on the Stock Exchange for the             出日期前五個營業日在聯交所錄得之平
      five business days immediately preceding the date of grant.                均收市價。

–     The period within which the options must be exercised would          －     本公司將於授予購股權時指定購股權須
      be specified by the Company at the time of grant. This period              予 行 使 之 期 限。此 屆 滿 期 限 須 不 遲 於 有
      must expire no later than 10 years from the relevant date of               關授予日期起計十年。

Independent auditor’s report to the members of                              獨立核數師報告
Vitasoy International Holdings Limited                                      致維他奶國際集團有限公司成員
(Incorporated in Hong Kong with limited liability)                         （於香港註冊成立的有限公司）


Opinion                                                                    意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Vitasoy           本 核 數 師（以 下 簡 稱「我 們」）已 審 計 列 載 於 第
International Holdings Limited (“the Company”) and its subsidiaries        120 至 254 頁的維他奶國際集團有限公司（以下
(“the Group”) set out on pages 120 to 254, which comprise the              簡稱「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集
consolidated statement of financial position as at 31st March              團」）的綜合財務報表，此財務報表包括於二零
2026, the consolidated statement of profit or loss, the consolidated       二六年三月三十一日的綜合財務狀況表與截至
statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income, the            該日止年度的綜合損益表、綜合損益及其他全
consolidated statement of changes in equity and the consolidated           面收益表、綜合權益變動表和綜合現金流量表，
cash flow statement for the year then ended and notes, comprising          以及附註，包括重大會計政策資料及其他解釋
material accounting policy information and other explanatory               資料。
information.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and      我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港會計
fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at        師公會頒布的《香港財務報告準則會計準則》真
31st March 2026 and of its consolidated financial performance and          實而中肯地反映了 貴集團於二零二六年三月
its consolidated cash flows for the year then ended in accordance          三十一日的綜合財務狀況及 貴集團截至該日
with HKFRS Accounting Standards as issued by the Hong Kong                 止年度的綜合財務表現及綜合現金流量，並已
Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”) and have been         遵照香港《公司條例》妥為擬備。
properly prepared in compliance with the Hong Kong Companies
Ordinance.


Basis for opinion                                                          意見的基礎
We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong                        我 們 已 根 據 香 港 會 計 師 公 會 頒 布 的《香 港 審 計
Standards on Auditing (“HKSAs”) as issued by the HKICPA. Our               準則》進行審計。我們在該等準則下承擔的責任
responsibilities under those standards are further described in the        已 在 本 報 告「核 數 師 就 審 計 綜 合 財 務 報 表 承 擔
Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of the consolidated financial     的責任」部分中作進一步闡述。根據香港會計師
statements section of our report. We are independent of the Group          公會頒布並適用於公眾利益實體財務報表審計
in accordance with the HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional            的《專業會計師道德守則》        （以下簡稱「守則」），
Accountants (“the Code”), as applicable to audits of financial             我們獨立於 貴集團。我們亦已履行守則中的
statements of public interest entities. We have also fulfilled our other   其他專業道德責任。我們相信，我們所獲得的
ethical responsibilities in accordance with the Code. We believe that      審計憑證能充足及適當地為我們的審計意見提
the audit evidence we have obtained is sufficient and appropriate to       供基礎。

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether          我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存

auditor’s report that includes our opinion. This report is made solely   我們是按照香港《公司條例》第 405 條的規定，
to you, as a body, in accordance with section 405 of the Hong Kong       僅向整體成員報告。除此以外，我們的報告不
Companies Ordinance, and for no other purpose. We do not assume          可用作其他用途。我們概不就本報告的內容，
responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person for the   對任何其他人士負責或承擔法律責任。
contents of this report.

can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,            引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能
individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected      影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴財務報表所作出
to influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of       的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。
these consolidated financial statements.

(b)    Basis of preparation of the financial statements                    (b)   財務報表之編製基準
The consolidated financial statements for the year ended 31st March        截至二零二六年三月三十一日止年度之綜合財
2026 comprise the Company and its subsidiaries (together referred          務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）
to as the “Group”) and the Group’s interest in a joint venture.            以及本集團於一間合營公司之權益。

The measurement basis used in the preparation of the financial             編製財務報表是以歷史成本作為計量基準。
statements is the historical cost basis.

The preparation of financial statements in conformity with HKFRS           管 理 層 須 在 編 製 符 合《香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 會 計
Accounting Standards requires management to make judgements,               準則》之財務報表時作出會影響會計政策應用，
estimates and assumptions that affect the application of policies          以及資產、負債、收入及支出之報告金額之判斷、
and reported amounts of assets, liabilities, income and expenses.          估計及假設。此等估計及相關假設是根據以往
The estimates and associated assumptions are based on historical           經驗和管理層因應當時情況認為合理之多項其
experience and various other factors that are believed to be               他因素作出，其結果構成當管理層在無法依循
reasonable under the circumstances, the results of which form the          其他途徑即時得知資產與負債之賬面值時所作
basis of making the judgements about carrying values of assets and         出判斷之基礎。實際結果可能有別於估計數額。
liabilities that are not readily apparent from other sources. Actual
results may differ from these estimates.

The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an                管理層會不斷審閱各項估計和相關假設。倘若
ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in         會計估計之修訂只影響某一期間，則該修訂便
the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects only   會在估計修訂期間內確認，或如果修訂對當期

     (e)    Joint venture                                                        (e)   合營公司
     A joint venture is an arrangement whereby the Group or Company              合營公司為本集團或本公司及其他人士按合約
     and other parties contractually agree to share control of the               同意下分配控制權及淨資產擁有權之安排。
     arrangement, and have rights to the net assets of the arrangement.

     An investment in a joint venture is accounted for in the consolidated       除合營公司的投資被分類為持作出售或納入被
     financial statements under the equity method, unless it is classified       分類為持作出售的出售組別外，於合營公司的
     as held for sale (or included in a disposal group that is classified as     投資是按權益法記入綜合財務報表，並且先以
     held for sale). Under the equity method, the investment is initially        成本入賬，另調整本集團於收購日應佔該投資
     recorded at cost, adjusted for any excess of the Group’s share of the       的可辨別淨資產之公允值所超出成本之任何金
     acquisition-date fair values of the investee’s identifiable net assets      額（如有）。投資成本包括購買價格、直接歸屬
     over the cost of the investment (if any). The cost of the investment        於收購投資之其他成本及對構成本集團股權投
     includes purchase price, other costs directly attributable to the           資一部分之合營公司之任何直接投資。往後，
     acquisition of the investment, and any direct investment into the           需調整在收購後本集團應佔該投資淨資產之變
     joint venture that forms part of the Group’s equity investment.             動及在附註 1(j)(ii) 所載有關該投資的減值損失。
     Thereafter, the investment is adjusted for the post acquisition change      任何超出收購日之成本、本集團應佔該投資收
     in the Group’s share of the investee’s net assets and any impairment        購後和已除稅的業績及年度內的任何減值損失
     loss relating to the investment (see note 1(j)(ii)). Any acquisition-       均在綜合損益表內確認，而本集團應佔該投資
     date excess over cost, the Group’s share of the post-acquisition,           收購後和已除稅之其他全面收益項目則在綜合
     post-tax results of the investees and any impairment losses for the         損益及其他全面收益表內確認。
     year are recognised in the consolidated statement of profit or loss,

(e)    Joint venture (continued)                                             (e)   合營公司（續）
When the Group’s share of losses exceeds its interest in the joint           當本集團對合營公司承擔的虧損額超過其所佔
venture, the Group’s interest is reduced to nil and recognition of           權益時，本集團所佔權益便會減少至零，並且
further losses is discontinued except to the extent that the Group           不再確認額外虧損；但如本集團須履行法定或

(j)    Credit loss and impairment of assets                                    (j)    信貸虧損及資產減值
(i)    Credit loss from financial instruments                                  (i)    金融工具之信貸虧損
The Group recognises a loss allowance for expected credit losses               本 集 團 為 按 攤 銷 成 本 計 量 之 金 融 資 產（包 括 現
(“ECLs”) on financial assets measured at amortised cost (including             金及現金等值項目以及應收賬款及其他應收款）
cash and cash equivalents, and trade and other receivables).                   確認預期信貸虧損之虧損撥備。

Measurement of ECLs                                                            預期信貸虧損之計量
ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of credit losses. Credit              預期信貸虧損乃信貸虧損之概率加權估計。信
losses are measured as the present value of all expected cash                  貸 虧 損 以 所 有 預 期 現 金 差 額（即 根 據 合 約 應 付
shortfalls (i.e. the difference between the cash flows due to the              予本集團之現金流量與本集團預期收取之現金
Group in accordance with the contract and the cash flows that the              流量之間的差額）的現值計量。
Group expects to receive).

Where the effect of discounting is material, the expected cash                 倘折現之影響屬重大，則預期現金差額將採用
shortfalls are discounted using the effective interest rate determined         應收賬款及其他應收款初始確認時釐定之實際
at initial recognition or an approximation thereof for trade and other         利率或其近似值折現。
receivables.

The maximum period considered when estimating ECLs is the                      估計預期信貸虧損時所考慮之最長期間為本集
maximum contractual period over which the Group is exposed to                  團面臨信貸風險之最長合約期間。
credit risk.

     Measurement of ECLs (continued)                                              預期信貸虧損之計量（續）
     In measuring ECLs, the Group takes into account reasonable and               在計量預期信貸虧損時，本集團考慮合理及有
     supportable information that is available without undue cost                 理據而無需付出過多的成本或努力獲得之資料。
     or effort. This includes information about past events, current              這包括有關過往事件、當前狀況及未來經濟狀
     conditions and forecasts of future economic conditions.                      況預測的資料。

     ECLs are measured on either of the following bases:                          預期信貸虧損基於下列其中一個基準計量：

     –      12-month ECLs: these are losses that are expected to result           －     十 二 個 月 之 預 期 信 貸 虧 損：預 期 於 報 告
            from possible default events within the 12 months after the                 日期後十二個月內可能發生之違約事件
            reporting date; and                                                         而導致之虧損；及

     –      lifetime ECLs: these are losses that are expected to result from      －     可 使 用 年 期 內 之 預 期 信 貸 虧 損：預 期 於
            all possible default events over the expected lives of the items            採用預期信貸虧損模式之項目在預期可
            to which the ECL model applies.                                             使用年期內所有可能發生之違約事件而
                                                                                        導致之虧損。

     Loss allowance for trade and other receivables is always measured            應收賬款及其他應收款之虧損撥備一般按相等
     at an amount equal to lifetime ECL. ECLs are estimated using                 於可使用年期內之預期信貸虧損之金額計量。
     a provision matrix based on the Group’s historical credit loss               預期信貸虧損乃使用基於本集團過往信貸虧損
     experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors and        經驗之撥備矩陣進行估算，並就於報告日期債
     an assessment of both the current and forecast general economic              務人之特定因素以及對當前及預測整體經濟狀
     conditions at the reporting date.                                            況之評估予以調整。

     Write-off policy                                                              撇銷政策
     The gross carrying amount of a financial asset is written off (either         倘金融資產並無實際收回前景，則其賬面總值
     partially or in full) to the extent that there is no realistic prospect of   （部分或全部）將會被撇銷。該情況通常出現在
     recovery. This is generally the case when the Group determines that           本集團認為債務人並無資產或收入來源可產生
     the debtor does not have assets or sources of income that could               足夠現金流量以償還將予撇銷之金額。
     generate sufficient cash flows to repay the amounts subject to the
     write-off.

–      Calculation of recoverable amount                                      －      計算可收回金額

       The recoverable amount of an asset is the greater of its fair                 資產之可收回金額為其公允值減去出售
       value less costs of disposal and value in use. In assessing value             成 本 及 使 用 價 值 兩 者 中 之 較 高 者。於 評
       in use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted to                     估 使 用 價 值 時，會 使 用 除 稅 前 折 現 率 將
       their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects               估 計 未 來 現 金 流 量 折 現 至 其 現 值。該 折
       current market assessments of time value of money and the                     現率反映市場當時所評估之貨幣時間價
       risks specific to the asset. Where an asset does not generate                 值 和 該 資 產 之 獨 有 風 險。倘 個 別 資 產 所
       cash inflows largely independent of those from other assets,                  產生之現金流入基本上不能獨立於其他
       the recoverable amount is determined for the smallest group                   資 產 所 產 生 之 現 金 流 入，則 就 獨 立 產 生
       of assets that generates cash inflows independently (i.e. a                   現金流入之最小資產組合（即現金產生單
       cash-generating unit (“CGU”)).                                                位）釐定可收回金額。

       An impairment loss is recognised in profit or loss if the                     資產或其所屬之現金產生單位之賬面值
       carrying amount of an asset, or the CGU to which it belongs,                  高 於 其 可 收 回 金 額 時，則 會 於 損 益 確 認
       exceeds its recoverable amount. Impairment losses recognised                  減 值 虧 損。就 現 金 產 生 單 位 確 認 之 減 值
       in respect of CGUs are allocated first to reduce the carrying                 虧損會首先分配予削減已分配至該現金
       amount of any goodwill allocated to the CGU (or group of                      產生單位（或一組單位）之任何商譽之賬
       units) and then, to reduce the carrying amount of the other                   面值，然後按比例削減該單位（或一組單
       assets in the unit (or group of units) on a pro rata basis, except            位）內其他資產之賬面值，惟資產賬面值
       that the carrying value of an asset will not be reduced below                 不可下調至低於其個別公允值減去出售
       its individual fair value less costs of disposal (if measurable), or          成本（如能計量）或使用價值（如能釐定）。
       value in use (if determinable).

     (k)     Inventories                                                         (k)     存貨
     Inventories are carried at the lower of cost and net realisable value.      存貨按成本值及可變現淨值兩者中之較低者入賬。

     Cost is calculated using the weighted average method and comprises          成本值乃以加權平均方法計算，並包括所有購
     all costs of purchase, costs of conversion and other costs incurred in      貨成本、加工成本及將存貨運往其現時地點及
     bringing the inventories to their present location and condition.           達至現有狀態之其他成本。

     Net realisable value is the estimated selling price in the ordinary         可變現淨值指正常業務過程中之估計售價減去
     course of business less the estimated costs of completion and the           完成交易之估計成本及進行銷售所需之估計成本。
     estimated costs necessary to make the sale.

     When inventories are sold, the carrying amount of those inventories         出售存貨時，其賬面值於有關收入確認期內確
     is recognised as an expense in the period in which the related              認為開支。任何存貨金額撇減至可變現淨值及
     revenue is recognised. The amount of any write-down of inventories          存貨之所有虧損均於撇減或虧損之發生期內確
     to net realisable value and all losses of inventories are recognised as     認為開支。倘存貨之撇減出現任何撥回，則於
     an expense in the period the write-down or loss occurs. The amount          撥回出現期內將費用作減額確認。
     of any reversal of any write-down of inventories is recognised as a
     reduction in the amount of inventories recognised as an expense in
     the period in which the reversal occurs.

money is material, provisions are stated at the present value of the        之現值計提撥備。
expenditure expected to settle the obligation.

     attaching to them. Grants that compensate the Group for expenses             支產生之同一期間有系統地於損益中確認為收
     incurred are recognised as income in profit or loss on a systematic          入。補償本集團資產成本之補助乃於資產之賬
     basis in the same periods in which the expenses are incurred.                面值中扣除，其後於該項資產之可使用期間以
     Grants that compensate the Group for the cost of an asset are                減少折舊開支之方式於損益中實際確認。
     deducted from the carrying amount of the asset and consequently
     are effectively recognised in profit or loss over the useful life of the
     asset by way of reduced depreciation expense.

     (v)    Borrowing costs                                                      (v)   借貸成本
     Borrowing costs that are directly attributable to the acquisition,          倘一項資產需較長時間才可準備就緒用作預定
     construction or production of an asset which necessarily takes a            用途或出售狀態，則直接歸屬於收購、興建或
     substantial period of time to get ready for its intended use or sale are    生產該項資產之借貸成本將被資本化為該項資
     capitalised as part of the cost of that asset. Other borrowing costs        產之成本之一部份。其他借貸成本在產生當期
     are expensed in the period in which they are incurred.                      列作支出。

     The capitalisation of borrowing costs as part of the cost of a              當資產開支及借貸成本已經產生，且為使資產
     qualifying asset commences when expenditure for the asset is                可用作擬定用途或可出售狀態所必要之活動已

     that are necessary to prepare the asset for its intended use or sale        成本之一部份。倘為使合資格資產可用作擬定
     are in progress. Capitalisation of borrowing costs is suspended or          用途或可出售狀態所必需之大部份活動中止或
     ceases when substantially all the activities necessary to prepare           完成，借貸成本之資本化則隨之中止或停止。
     the qualifying asset for its intended use or sale are interrupted or
     complete.

11 Property, plant and equipment (continued)                              11 物業、廠房及設備（續）

(d)    The fair value measurement                                         (d)   公允值計量
The fair value measurement is categorised as a Level 3 valuation          公允值計量根據《香港財務報告準則》第 13 號「公
under the three-level fair value hierarchy as defined in HKFRS            允值計量 」所 界 定 之 三 級 公 允 值 架 構 分 類 為 第
13, Fair value measurement . The level into which a fair value            三級估值。將某公允值計量分類之等級乃經參
measurement is classified is determined with reference to                 考如下估值方法所用數據之可觀察性及重要性
observability and significance of the inputs used in the valuation        後釐定：
technique as follows:

–      Level 1 valuations: Fair value measured using only Level 1         －     第一級估值：僅使用第一級輸入數據（即
       inputs i.e. unadjusted quoted prices in active markets for               於計量日同類資產或負債於活躍市場之
       identical assets or liabilities at the measurement date;                 未經調整報價）計量之公允值；

–      Level 2 valuations: Fair value measured using Level 2 inputs       －     第二級估值：使用第二級輸入數據（即未
       i.e. observable inputs which fail to meet Level 1, and not using         能達到第一級之可觀察輸入數據）且並未
       significant unobservable inputs. Unobservable inputs are                 使用重大不可觀察輸入數據計量之公允
       inputs for which market data are not available; and                      值。不 可 觀 察 輸 入 數 據 為 無 市 場 數 據 之
                                                                                輸入數據；及

–      Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using significant          －     第 三 級 估 值：使 用 重 大 不 可 觀 察 輸 入 數
       unobservable inputs.                                                     據計量之公允值。

(i)   Investment properties                                               (i)   投資物業

     overall cap of $390,000 per employee. Currently, the Group does

     obligation.

     In June 2022, the Hong Kong SAR Government (the “Government”)             於二零二二年六月，香港特別行政區政府（「政
     gazetted the Hong Kong Employment and Retirement Scheme                   府」）刊 憲《2022 年 僱 傭 及 退 休 計 劃 法 例（抵 銷

     2025 (the “Transition Date”). The Amendment Ordinance abolish             條例廢除僱主可利用其向強積金計劃作出之強

     the MPF scheme. Separately, the Government introduced a subsidy           以協助僱主。
     scheme to assist employers after the abolition.

     to reduce the LSP in respect of an employee’s service from the            服務金。然而，倘僱員於過渡日期前已開始受僱，

     As part of the restructuring of the Group in February 1994, the            作為本集團於一九九四年二月進行之重組之一
     Company disposed of a property to a former subsidiary and                  部份，本公司向一間前附屬公司出售一項物業，

     A total gain, representing the difference between the historical           生總收益，即所出售物業之歷史賬面值與已收

     consideration received, resulted from such transaction.

     value of the property received (“the property”). The unrealised gain       現 收 益 已 計 入 資 本 儲 備，並 於 計 算 物 業 折 舊
     was taken to capital reserve and is realised on depreciation of the        時 變 現。於 年 內，1,218,000 元（二 零 二 五 年：
     property. During the year, $1,218,000 (2025: $1,218,000) was               1,218,000 元）自資本儲備轉撥至保留溢利。

which are recognised directly in equity at cost. No gain or loss is        本 直 接 於 權 益 中 確 認。於 購 買、出 售、發 行 或
recognised in the statement of profit or loss on the purchase, sale,       註銷本集團自有股權工具時，概不會於損益表
issue or cancellation of the Group’s own equity instruments.               中確認任何收益或虧損。

(g)    Capital management                                                  (g)    資本管理
The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital are to                本集團在管理資本時之首要目標為保障本集團
safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern, so that      能持續經營的能力，透過將產品及服務的價格
it can continue to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for       訂於與風險相稱的水平及按合理成本籌措融資，
other stakeholders, by pricing products and services commensurately        使其能繼續為股東提供回報及為其他利益相關
with the level of risk and by securing access to finance at a              人士提供利益。
reasonable cost.

The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages its capital           本集團積極及定期對資本架構進行檢討及管理，
structure to maintain a balance between the higher shareholder             以在較高股東回報可能附帶較高借貸水平與雄
returns that might be possible with higher levels of borrowings and        厚資本狀況帶來之優勢及保障間取得平衡，並
the advantages and security afforded by a sound capital position,          因應經濟環境之變化對資本架構作出調整。
and makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of changes in
economic conditions.

Consistent with industry practices, the Group monitors its capital         本著業內慣例，本集團以負債對經調整資本比
structure on the basis of a debt-to-adjusted capital ratio. For this       率作為監控其資本架構之基準。就此目的，本
purpose, the Group defines debt as the total of bills payable, bank        集團將負債界定為應付票據、銀行貸款及租賃
loans and lease liabilities, and adjusted capital as all components of     負債之總額，經調整資本為權益之所有組成部
equity less unaccrued proposed dividends.                                  份減非累計擬派股息。

The Group’s strategy, which was unchanged from the year ended              本集團之策略與截至二零二五年三月三十一日
31st March 2025, was to maintain the debt-to-adjusted capital ratio        止年度保持不變，將負債對經調整資本比率維
below 30%. In order to maintain or adjust the ratio, the Group may         持 於 30% 以 下。為 保 持 或 調 整 有 關 比 率，本 集
adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders, issue new             團或會調整向股東派付之股息金額、發行新股

assets to reduce debt.                                                     資產以減低負債。

Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency risks arises in   本集團在一般業務過程中存在信貸、流動資金、
the normal course of the Group’s business. The Group’s exposures to         利率及貨幣風險。本集團面臨之有關風險及本

(a)    Credit risk                                                          (a)   信貸風險
Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default on its      信貸風險指交易方違反其合約責任而導致本集
contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to the Group.         團財務虧損之風險。

The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable to trade and other        本集團之信貸風險主要來自應收賬款及其他應
receivables. Management has a credit policy in place and exposure to        收款。管理層備有信貸政策，而所面臨之有關
credit risk is monitored on an ongoing basis.                               信貸風險持續受到監控。

The Group’s cash and bank deposits are placed with financial                本集團之現金及銀行存款乃存放於有良好信貸
institutions with sound credit ratings, and the management consider         評級之金融機構，管理層認為本集團面臨之信
the Group’s exposure to credit risk is low.                                 貸風險為低。

The Group does not provide any guarantees which would expose the            本集團並無提供任何將令本集團面臨信貸風險
Group to credit risk.                                                       之擔保。

Trade debtors and bills receivable (which are included in trade             應收賬款及應收票據（已包括於應收賬款
and other receivables)                                                      及其他應收款內）

requiring credit over a certain amount. These evaluations focus on          別信貸評估。該等評估集中於客戶過往之到期
the customer’s past history of making payments, when due, and               還款記錄及現時還款能力，並會考慮客戶之特
current ability to pay, and take into account information specific to       定資料以及有關客戶營運所在之經濟環境。應
the customer as well as pertaining to the economic environment in           收賬款及應收票據一般於發單日起計一至三個
which the customer operates. Trade debtors and bills receivables are        月內到期。一般而言，本集團不會從客戶取得
generally due within one to three months from the date of billing.          抵押品。
Normally, the Group does not obtain collateral from customers.

industries or countries in which the customers operate. Significant          無重大集中信貸風險。重大集中信貸風險主要

instruments of the Group are insensitive to any change in market           率變動並不敏感。匯報日之利率變動並不影響
interest rate. A change in interest rate at the end of the reporting       損益。
period would not affect profit or loss.

(d)    Currency risk                                                       (d)    貨幣風險
The Group is exposed to currency risk primarily through sales,             本集團面臨之貨幣風險主要來自因買賣及借貸
purchases and borrowings which give rise to receivables, payables          而產生之應收款、應付款及現金結餘，該等項
and cash balances that are denominated in a foreign currency, i.e.         目 乃 按 外 幣（即 交 易 所 涉 業 務 之 功 能 貨 幣 以 外
a currency other than the functional currency of the operations to         之貨幣）計值。產生有關風險之貨幣主要為美元、
which the transactions relate. The currencies giving rise to this risk     澳幣、坡幣及人民幣。
are primarily United States dollars (“USD”), Australian dollars (“AUD”),
Singapore dollars (“SGD”) and Renminbi (“RMB”).

For group entities whose functional currency is Hong Kong dollars          就功能貨幣為港幣之集團實體而言，除若干與
(“HKD”), all sales and purchases are denominated in either HKD             集團實體進行之交易及採購以澳幣、坡幣及人
or USD, except for certain transactions with group entities and            民幣計值外，所有買賣均按港幣或美元計值。
purchases that are denominated in AUD, SGD and RMB. Given                  由於港幣與美元掛鈎，管理層預期有關該等以
the HKD is pegged to the USD, management does not expect that              美元計值之交易並無任何重大貨幣風險。
there will be any significant currency risk associated with such USD
denominated transactions.

For group entities whose functional currency is a currency other than      就功能貨幣為港幣以外之貨幣之集團實體而言，
HKD, except for certain sales and transactions with group entities         除若干出售及與集團實體進行之交易以及與集
and certain borrowings from group entities that are denominated            團實體之若干借貸按港幣計值外，其他大部份
in HKD, most of the other transactions are denominated in their            交易均按其功能貨幣計值。
functional currencies.

The Group’s policies for managing such risk were unchanged from            自截至二零二五年三月三十一日止年度起，本
the year ended 31st March 2025.                                            集團管理有關風險之政策並無變動。

     30 Non-adjusting events after the reporting                                30 毋須作出調整之匯報日後事項

     HKFRS 18, Presentation and disclosure in financial                       《香港財務報告準則》第 18 號
     statements                                                               「財務報表呈列及披露」
     HKFRS 18 will replace HKAS 1 Presentation of financial statements        《香港財務報告準則》第 18 號將取代《香港會計
     and aims to improve the transparency and comparability of                 準則》第 1 號「財務報表的呈列」，旨在提高實體
     information about an entity’s financial statements. HKFRS 18 is          財務報表資訊之透明度及可比性。《香港財務報
     effective for annual reporting periods beginning on or after 1st         告準則》第 18 號於二零二七年一月一日或之後
     January 2027 and is to be applied retrospectively.                       開始之年度報告期間生效，並須追溯應用。

     Among other changes, under HKFRS 18, entities are required to            除其他變動外，根據《香港財務報告準則》第 18
     classify all income and expenses into five categories in the statement   號，實體須於損益表中將所有收入及開支分類
     of profit or loss, namely the operating, investing, financing,           為 五 個 類 別，即 經 營、投 資、融 資、已 終 止 經
     discontinued operations and income tax categories. Entities are also     營業務及所得稅類別。實體亦須於財務報表之
     required to provide specific disclosures about management-defined        單一附註中提供有關管理層定義之業績指標之
     performance measures in a single note in the financial statements.       特定披露。

     The Group does not plan to early adopt HKFRS 18 and is still in the      本集團不計劃提早採用《香港財務報告準則》第
     process of assessing the impact of the adoption.                         18 號，目前仍在評估採用該準則之影響。
